### [1] [Quantum Cosmology at the Cusp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40763)
*Luis A. Anchordoqui,Alek Bedroya,Muldrow Etheredge,Dieter Lüst,Severin Lüst,Alexander Westphal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了顶向下量子引力构造中saxionic方向导致的模空间尖点，分析了轴子支持的标度宇宙学解，并利用Wheeler-DeWitt方程证明在特定条件下系统会演化至该解；同时讨论了加速膨胀与Trans-Planckian禁令之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解顶向下量子引力中模空间尖点处的宇宙学行为，特别是轴子动力学对宇宙演化的影响，以及其与加速膨胀和量子引力约束（如Trans-Planckian禁令）的关系。

Method: 基于标量势和轴子衰变常数在尖点处的渐近形式（V∼e^{-αφ}, f∼e^{-γφ}），结合空间紧致宇宙中轴子动量量子化特性，运用Wheeler-DeWitt方程分析半经典态的晚时演化行为。

Result: 发现当(d−2)α(α+2γ)>4(d−1)时，轴子动能驱动系统趋向轴子支持的标度解；推导出相变时间上限；长期加速膨胀要求2γ>(d−2)α，但该条件与Trans-Planckian禁令及磁荷膜质量约束（2γ≤(d−2)α）矛盾。

Conclusion: 轴子在模空间尖点的动力学虽可支持快速膨胀，但与基本量子引力约束存在本质冲突，暗示此类构造可能面临严重理论限制。

Abstract: In top-down quantum gravity constructions, saxionic directions typically lead to cusps in moduli space, where the scalar potential and the axion decay constant asymptotically behave as $V\sim e^{-αφ}$ and $f\sim e^{-γφ}$ in reduced Planck units. Such cusps admit axion-supported scaling cosmologies that expand faster than the corresponding solutions with the axion held fixed, for the same scalar potential. In spatially compact universes, axionic momentum is quantized, and any semiclassical state in the cusp with a localized axion carries nonzero axionic kinetic energy. We show using the Wheeler-DeWitt equation that this contribution drives the late-time evolution towards the axion-supported scaling solution whenever $(d-2)α(α+2γ)>4(d-1)$ in $d$ spacetime dimensions. We derive an upper bound on the transition time. Long-lived accelerated expansion requires $2γ>(d-2)α$. The trans-Planckian censorship conjecture requires the opposite inequality, $2γ\leq(d-2)α$, which also follows independently from requiring the brane magnetically charged under the axion to remain heavier than the Hubble scale.

</details>


### [2] [Guaranteed Slater Conditions for the Numerical Conformal Bootstrap: Fixing Instabilities in SDPB, Navigator Methods, and Skydiving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40764)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套‘Slater工具’，用于诊断和修复数值共形自举等半定规划（SDP）中因违反Slater条件而导致的收敛失败问题，包括诊断退化、构造严格可行点的变换、以及基于暴露证书的面缩减（facial reduction）方法，并在多个典型自举问题（如3d Ising、navigator、电流-标量）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自举问题中的半定规划（SDP）违反了保证内点法收敛所需的原始与对偶Slater条件，导致收敛缓慢、结果不确定甚至Skydive算法崩溃，亟需系统性诊断与修复机制。

Method: 提出‘Slater工具’：1）诊断工具——量化Slater边界并提取暴露证书以识别退化；2）变换方法——提供原SDP目标的严格上下界，特定参数下可达精确值；3）面缩减（facial reduction）——利用暴露证书直接消除退化；所有工具均兼容SDPB的PMP格式。

Result: 成功复现、解释并修复了混合关联子3d Ising自举中所有已知失效类型；证实X-Slater条件在可行性模式下总失效、在navigator问题中普遍成立且可由导航函数附加条件保证；揭示‘扭度间隙’经验补救法本质是消除正性约束端点处的退化；完整分类并消除了已发表电流-标量自举中全部退化（含x=∞处不可由间隙假设修复的多项式次数不匹配退化）。

Conclusion: Slater条件失效是自举SDP实践中的核心障碍，‘Slater工具’提供了统一、严谨且实用的诊断与修复框架，显著提升数值自举的鲁棒性与可靠性，并深化了对退化来源（如块预因子误分解、等/不等算符对混叠、多项式次数不匹配）的理论理解。

Abstract: The semidefinite programs (SDPs) of the numerical conformal bootstrap, and of S-matrix and quantum-mechanics bootstraps, are solved with primal-dual interior-point methods whose convergence guarantees rest on primal and dual Slater conditions: the existence of strictly feasible points on both sides of the duality. Many bootstrap SDPs violate these. The consequences range from slow convergence to inconclusive verdicts and the breakdown of the Skydive algorithm. We introduce "Slater tools", all acting within the PMP class accepted by SDPB. Diagnostics measure the Slater margins and, when a margin vanishes, extract the exposing certificate that identifies the degeneracy. Two independent cures follow. Transforms that provide rigorous bounds on the original SDP objective and that for the right parameter choices return the exact value. Facial reductions that use the exposing certificate to remove the degeneracies themselves. We reproduce, explain and resolve all known types of failures in a mixed-correlator 3d Ising setup. We show that the $X$-Slater condition (SDPB's primal) fails at every disallowed point in feasibility mode, holds in every navigator problem we tested, and can be guaranteed by one additional condition on the navigator function. We show that the folklore "instability remedy" of a twist gap works by removing degeneracies at the endpoints of the positivity conditions. These often stem from a mistake in factoring out the block prefactor but can also arise legitimately in channels that mix equal and unequal operator pairs. Finally we classify and remove all degeneracies of the published current-scalar bootstrap, among them one at $x=\infty$, thus incurable by any gap assumption, caused by unequal polynomial degrees within one matrix block.

</details>


### [3] [The running of Weyl gauged gravity: asymptotic freedom, its obstruction, and a way out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40769)
*Diego Buccio,Fabrizio Chicconi,Luca Parente,Omar Zanusso*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Weyl共形几何框架下计算了二次引力的一圈β函数，发现Weyl张量耦合与Weyl规范耦合具有渐近自由性，但由Weyl协变曲率标量平方（\widehat{R}^2）主导的耦合流缺乏物理区域内的紫外不动点；引入足够大的Weyl不变四导数标量耦合可消除该障碍，获得全方向紫外吸引且Weyl规范耦合非零的不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二次引力理论在Weyl共形几何下的紫外可重整性与可能的紫外完备性，特别是理解其β函数行为及是否存在合适的紫外不动点。

Method: 采用背景场方法进行图解发散提取，并针对微分同胚与尺度变换构成的半直积规范群设计适配的规范固定程序，计算一阶圈图修正下的β函数。

Result: Weyl张量耦合与Weyl规范耦合渐近自由；\widehat{R}^2对应耦合无物理区域内的紫外不动点；加入标准模型或一般两导数物质后结论不变；引入足够强的Weyl不变四导数标量耦合可产生全方向紫外吸引、非零Weyl规范耦合的物理不动点。

Conclusion: 纯二次引力在Weyl共形几何中难以实现紫外完备，但通过添加特定Weyl不变高维标量项可恢复紫外可接受性，为构建一致量子引力提供新路径。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop beta functions of quadratic gravity in Weyl conformal geometry, the gauge theory of diffeomorphisms and dilatations with a dynamical Weyl gauge boson. The divergences are extracted diagrammatically in the background field method, with a gauge fixing procedure adapted to the semidirect product structure of the gauge group. The Weyl-tensor coupling and the Weyl gauge coupling are asymptotically free, and the running of the latter is special, being independent of the other couplings and of any two-derivative matter content. The obstruction to an ultraviolet completion comes from the square of a Weyl-covariant curvature scalar, denoted $\widehat{R}{}^2$: the flow of the corresponding coupling has no fixed point in the physically interesting region and becomes trivial, the only real fixed points require a decoupled Weyl boson and a tachyonic scalar mode. This also holds when including the Standard Model with any nonminimal Higgs coupling and, more generally, for any matter with two-derivative kinetic terms. Weyl-invariant four-derivative scalars with a sufficiently large coupling to $\widehat{R}{}^2$ can remove the obstruction, producing a fixed point in the physical region that is ultraviolet attractive in all directions and has a nonvanishing Weyl gauge coupling.

</details>


### [4] [DiscKosky: Efficient Genealogical Constraints from Sector-By-Sector Critical Point Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40772)
*Giulio Crisanti,Luke Lippstreu,Andrew J. McLeod,Maria Polackova*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DiscKosky——一个用于高效生成费曼积分顺序不连续性谱系约束的新Mathematica软件包，其核心是通过追踪临界点方程解的消失来关联Landau奇点与壳上区域，并利用层级原理排除不兼容的顺序不连续性；该方法在高维临界流形出现时虽偶有失效，但反而可用于识别只需计算一次的非重复不连续性，并已成功应用于多类两圈五点、三圈费曼积分及宇宙学关联子中的欧拉积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决费曼积分顺序不连续性的系统性生成与约束问题，特别是对复杂多圈、多点积分的解析结构进行高效分析，亟需一种能自动识别Landau奇点及其相容性关系的工具。

Method: DiscKosky通过追踪费曼积分对应临界点方程的解在运动学极限下的存在性变化，将每个Landau奇点关联到一组壳上传播子；再依据层级原理，禁止涉及不兼容壳上传播子集合的顺序不连续性；对高维临界流形情形，则利用其异常行为识别非重复不连续性。

Result: 实现了DiscKosky软件包，成功应用于两圈五点（含1–2个质量腿）及前沿三圈费曼积分，并拓展至宇宙学关联子中的广义欧拉积分，验证了其在解析结构探测上的有效性与普适性。

Conclusion: DiscKosky为研究费曼积分及更广义欧拉积分的解析性质提供了强大新工具，不仅提升了顺序不连续性预测的效率与可靠性，还揭示了高维临界现象与非重复不连续性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We present DiscKosky, a new Mathematica package for efficiently generating genealogical constraints on the sequential discontinuities of Feynman integrals. DiscKosky works by tracking the solutions to the critical point equations associated with a Feynman integral; by identifying which of these critical points cease to exist in a given kinematic limit, we can associate each Landau singularity with a set of on-shell sectors. The hierarchical principle then forbids sequential discontinuities with respect to singularities that are associated with incompatible sets of on-shell propagators. In rare cases, this algorithm fails to make predictions due to the appearance of higher-dimensional critical varieties---nevertheless, we show that this phenomenon can sometimes be used to identify non-repeating discontinuities, which can be computed at most once in a Feynman integral. We illustrate the power of DiscKosky by applying the package to a broad class of Feynman integrals, including two-loop five-point integrals with one and two massive legs, and three-loop examples that go beyond the current computational frontier. DiscKosky can also be used to probe the analytic properties of more general classes of Euler integrals, which we illustrate by considering several examples that arise in the context of cosmological correlators.

</details>

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在AdS5×SE5型IIB背景中对探测D3膜进行量子化，推导出巨引力子对超共形指标的贡献，并验证了其与对偶场论预测的一致性，为引力侧直接解决超对称微态计数问题提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在超引力侧直接解决超对称微态计数问题，而非依赖对偶场论，探索巨引力子展开的普适世界面起源。

Method: 在AdS5×SE5（特别是T1,1和Yp,q）背景下，对极大巨引力子的D3膜进行量子化，计算其标量、矢量和费米子涨落，并提取BPS谱。

Result: 所得单字母指标与对偶环面quiver规范理论的巨引力子展开预测一致，证实单巨引力子项可直接由量子化D3膜自由度复现。

Conclusion: 结果表明巨引力子展开具有普适的世界面起源，支持构建一种内在引力框架（类似开-闭-开三重性）来处理超对称微态计数问题。

Abstract: We derive giant-graviton contributions to superconformal indices by quantizing probe D3-branes in type IIB backgrounds $\mathrm{AdS}_{5}\times SE_{5}$. For $SE_{5}=T^{1,1}$ and $Y^{p,q}$, we determine the scalar, vector, and fermionic fluctuations of maximal giant gravitons and extract their BPS spectra. The resulting single-letter indices agree with those predicted by the giant-graviton expansions of the dual toric quiver gauge theories. These results show that the single-giant terms in finite-$N$ supersymmetric counting functions can be recovered directly from quantized D3-brane degrees of freedom and point toward a universal worldvolume origin of giant-graviton expansions directly on the supergravity side. We view the results as a step in an intrinsically gravitational framework, much like the open-closed-open triality, for addressing supersymmetric microstate-counting problems that are conventionally outsourced to the dual field theory.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解强耦合量子场论中边界态的结构及其在弦论几何工程背景下的物理含义，特别是边界态之间可否通过孤子剖面连续连接，以及该性质如何反映目标空间几何（如非正规化模态）和对偶结构。

Method: 基于边界态格点引入边界筛选群B，将其定义为不能通过孤子/扭结型跃迁相互连接的边界态等价类；结合弦论中世界面CFT与目标空间QFT的对应，将B与缺陷群D类比；分析界面与多QFT连接情形下的守恒荷起源；以非几何Landau-Ginzburg模型及其4D提升为具体示例进行验证。

Result: 边界筛选群B是非零时可诊断目标空间存在非正规化模态；界面/连接处必然携带由孤子吸收/透射诱导的非平凡守恒荷，从而无法完全消失；在世界面CFT语境下，B即为缺陷群D的世界面类比；该框架适用于非几何超对称模型。

Abstract: We present a coarse classification scheme for the boundary data of massive QFTs based on transitions of boundary states via soliton / kink profiles. Starting from the lattice of boundary states, we introduce the boundary screening group $\mathbb{B}$: the equivalence classes of boundary states which cannot be connected by such transitions. In string theory backgrounds which geometrically engineer an interacting QFT in the target space, $\mathbb{B}$ computes the worldsheet analog of the target space defect group $\mathbb{D}$. When $\mathbb{B}$ is non-zero, it provides a diagnostic for having a target space with non-normalizable modes. For interfaces between QFTs, similar considerations imply the existence of a non-trivial conserved charge associated with soliton absorption / transmission, which means that the interface cannot fully dissolve / disappear. Similar considerations hold for junctions of QFTs, which in the special case of string worldsheet CFTs translate to junctions of string theories. We illustrate these general considerations with a number of non-geometric 2D $\mathcal{N} = (2,2)$ Landau-Ginzburg models, as well as their natural lifts to 4D $\mathcal{N} = 1$ Wess-Zumino models.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明l≥2标量/矢量模无指数不稳定；发现近视界处恢复普适Rindler谱（含声、剪切激发）；数值显示这些流体模随边界外移连续过渡至渐近AdS边界的准正规模，并在大黑洞极限下对应CFT的声与剪切极点；AdS黑膜情形亦呈现类似连续性。

Abstract: We study linearized gravitational perturbations of four-dimensional Schwarzschild-AdS black holes with finite timelike boundaries subject to conformal boundary conditions. These fix the conformal class of the induced metric and the mean curvature, while allowing the boundary Weyl mode to fluctuate. The photon sphere plays a distinguished role in this problem: a static boundary at this radius is totally umbilic, and the scalar problem separates exactly into independent bulk and boundary sectors. We prove the absence of exponentially growing scalar and vector modes for spherical harmonics of degree two and higher. We determine how large angular momentum instabilities are suppressed as the boundary approaches umbilicity. Near the horizon, we recover the universal Rindler spectrum, including sound and shear excitations. We numerically follow these fluid modes as the boundary is moved outwards and find that they approach standard gravitational quasinormal modes at the asymptotic AdS$_4$ boundary. For large black holes, these asymptotic endpoints correspond to the sound and shear poles of the dual three-dimensional conformal field theory. We also study perturbations of the AdS$_4$ black brane, where we find an analogous continuation of the fluid modes in the absence of an intermediate umbilic boundary.

</details>


### [8] [Monopole-Fermion Scattering from Twisted Conformal Boundary States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40779)

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过共形边界态在扭曲扇区中的重叠，推导了磁单极-费米子散射关联函数，揭示了Callan-Rubakov效应中电荷守恒所要求的出射激发，并利用扭曲边界态和四维分波散射建立联系，补全了s波对幺正性关系的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁单极子与费米子散射中的Callan-Rubakov效应，特别是电荷守恒所要求的非局域出射激发如何由共形边界条件自然产生。

Method: 基于对称性保持的边界条件，引入由拓扑对称线诱导的扭曲扇区；构造N个自由Dirac费米子在U(1)^N对称性保持下的扭曲边界态（推广至平移电荷格点）；结合簇分解与开闭对偶确定系数及归一化；将所得关联函数映射到四维分波散射，使用规范协变渐近态分析。

Result: 得到Callan-Rubakov边界条件下入射费米子与出射缺陷算符的两点函数，其系数模为1；证明该扭曲通道恰好提供幺正性关系中缺失的s波贡献，且归一化精确匹配。

Conclusion: 扭曲扇区的边界态为理解强耦合单极-费米子散射提供了严格共形框架，揭示了拓扑对称线与非局域物理响应之间的深刻联系，并解决了长期存在的幺正性s波缺失问题。

Abstract: We derive monopole-fermion scattering correlators from conformal boundary states in twisted sectors. The key ingredient is that symmetry-preserving boundary conditions contain sectors twisted by topological symmetry lines. Their overlaps determine correlators of operators attached to these lines, including the outgoing excitations required by charge conservation in the Callan-Rubakov effect. We construct the twisted boundary states of $N$ free Dirac fermions with $U(1)^N$-preserving boundary conditions, extending the familiar description in terms of charge lattices to shifted lattices describing twisted sectors. Using cluster decomposition and open-closed duality, we determine their coefficients and fix their normalization relative to the untwisted boundary state. The corner mode is given by the longitudinal electric field sourced by the matter outside the subregion and thus constitutes a centre for the regional algebra. Abstract: We present Hubble Space Telescope (HST) imaging of six Local Volume dwarf galaxies identified by the SEAMLESS survey - Cetus B, Hydrus A, Ursa Major C, Hydra B, Grapes, and M83-dw14 - along with Centaurus C (Cen C), discovered serendipitously in the M83-dw14 field. Our HST imaging resolves each system into individual stars, enabling measurements of tip of the red giant branch (TRGB) distances (3.6-6.5 Mpc), structural parameters, and star formation histories (SFHs). With accurate TRGB distances, we place each target in its environmental context, ranging from a confirmed satellite (M83-dw14) to isolated systems (Hydrus A, Hydra B, Grapes, Cen C). Cen C ($M_V=-7.96$) is among the faintest isolated dwarfs known. It matches the size and luminosity of Leo T, the lowest mass gas-rich galaxy currently known, yet unlike Leo T it shows neither detected HI ($\log(M_{\rm HI}/M_{\odot})<5.3$) nor recent star formation. Ursa Major C shows strongly asymmetric, spatially offset HI, suggesting that feedback may efficiently displace gas in such low-mass systems. Overall, our sample exhibits a broad range of SFHs and quenching states that does not track simply with present-day environment. This diversity is consistent with recent evidence that similarly varied SFHs occur even among isolated low-mass dwarfs, pointing to the important roles of inhomogeneous reionization, stochastic mass assembly, and internal feedback alongside environmental effects.

Abstract: We construct a generative network for Monte-Carlo sampling in lattice field theories and beyond, for which the layerwise propagation is computed and optimised independently on each layer. Thus, it transforms the generative task into that of solving once the set of independent and linear differential equations for the kernels of the transformation. As these equations are analytically known, the kernels can be refined iteratively. Abstract: Determining the meson spectrum directly from Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) remains a central challenge, since QCD is strongly coupled at low energies and most excited states appear as resonances in hadronic decay channels. The approximately thermal behavior observed in the light-nucleus spectra motivates the use of a perturbative series expansion of the $q$-Gaussian string tension fluctuations around $q=1$. The resulting $p_T$ spectrum is expressed as a perturbative series expansion around the thermal distribution with nonthermal corrections given by Gamma distributions, providing closed forms for the effective temperature, moments, and cumulants. In the superstatistical framework, the associated temperature fluctuations show that the medium in which light nuclei are produced is out of equilibrium. Additionally, we derive closed formulas for the coalescence parameter at arbitrary order, which agree well with experimental data. Abstract: We investigate topological inflation in minimal multi-natural inflation, in which the axion potential consists of two cosine terms. We also discuss a symmetry-breaking origin of the required domain walls when the inflaton is the angular component of a complex scalar field. Our results show that the observational tension encountered in the minimal quartic double-well model need not extend to topological inflation in a minimal multi-natural axion potential.

Abstract: We complete the calculation of the two-loop Feynman integrals required for the next-to-next-to-leading order QCD corrections to on-shell top--antitop production in association with a photon at hadron colliders, in the leading-colour approximation. These are largely in canonical form; the exceptions are a few entries, associated with elliptic curves or nested square roots, that depend at most quadratically on the dimensional regulator $ε$. We solve the differential equations in terms of a tailored set of special functions, which will enable the analytic extraction of the two-loop finite remainder. We evaluate these functions by numerically solving the differential equations they satisfy, and show that the resulting routine is fast and stable enough in the physical region for phenomenological applications.

Cosmological, direct and indirect detection bounds on MeV-scale mediators motivate a neutrinophilic scalar dilepton that decays invisibly without Higgs mixing. Abstract: As observations of the cosmic dawn (CD) and epoch of reionisation (EoR) rapidly improve in precision, the approximate forward models used to interpret them might struggle to keep pace. Bayesian inference with a misspecified model, one that cannot represent the processes generating the observations, can yield tightly constrained, yet misleading and biased astrophysics, with different parts of the observations favouring incompatible parameter values. Model misspecification can evade a $χ^2$ goodness-of-fit test, and Bayesian model selection requires a better model, which is not always available. We therefore apply the suspiciousness, a statistic developed to quantify tensions between datasets, to CD/EoR inference as a model criticism tool that tests whether these parts can be explained by the same parameters. We first apply it to HERA 21-cm power spectrum (PS) upper limits, which are too weak to reveal misspecification, as expected, and to HST and JWST UV luminosity functions (LFs). Under our galaxy model, the UV LFs at $z\leq10$ and $z>10$ are in tension at $5.0σ$ in the Gaussian limit, a failure visible in the fits, although doubling the uncertainties of the $z>10$ observations reduces it to $2.1σ$. Finally, we analyse a mock SKA-Low AA$^\ast$ 1080 h 21-cm PS with the model that generated it and with a simpler, misspecified model. We find that the misspecified model silently fails: it fits the PS well, passing visual inspection and, for most noise realisations, a $χ^2$ test, yet its inferred astrophysics is wrong. The Bayesian evidence disfavours it, but only relative to the true model, which is unavailable for real observations. The suspiciousness finds no tension for the well-specified model, but a $\sim3.4σ$ tension for the misspecified one, strongly ruling it out without requiring a more adequate candidate model.

</details>

Model-independent analyses are common for supernova datasets but rarely applied to other data. In this work, we use uncalibrated baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) from DESI DR2 for a model-independent test of acceleration, which makes no assumptions about the theory of gravity or the energy content of the universe. Abstract: We develop a continuous framework connecting quantum field fluctuations in the early Universe to the abundance and observable properties of galaxies and active galactic nuclei (AGN) at cosmic dawn. Beginning with quantum fields in curved Friedmann Lemaitre Robertson Walker spacetime, we follow inflationary vacuum fluctuations into the primordial curvature spectrum, linear matter power spectrum, mass variance $σ(MHz)$, mass peak height $ν(MHz)$, and the statistics of nonlinear halo formation. The Press-Schechter and Sheth-Tormen formalisms provide an analytic description of halo abundance and its dependence on peak rarity. A complementary high redshift halo mass function calibrated with the GUREFT simulations of Yung et al. (2024) provides a numerical description specifically constructed for the cosmic dawn regime probed by JWST. The comparison between these analytic and simulation calibrated prescriptions provides a direct measure of the sensitivity of predicted halo populations to the observations. We separate this cosmological supply of halos from the astrophysical and observational mappings that produce stars, ultraviolet luminosities, and active galactic nuclei. Controlled numerical experiments show that a percent level perturbation in the primordial fluctuation amplitude produces only a modest change in $σ$ and $ν$, but can strongly amplify the exponentially suppressed high $ν$ tail of the Halo Mass Function. This sensitivity increases toward high halo mass and high redshift, identifying rare cosmic dawn populations as potentially powerful probes of primordial statistics while simultaneously exposing the importance of uncertainties in halo abundance. High redshift AGN population statistics and the Ultraviolet Luminosity Function provide additional constraints on the primordial power spectrum and dynamics of primordial inflation theory.

Motivation: 为在大规模测光巡天中实现物理可解释、无需强分布假设且计算高效的星系分类与光度红移估计，尤其服务于Stage IV宇宙学分析。

Abstract: We present FOMO-Z (Flexible Optimizer of Multi-dimensional Operators and photo-$z$s), a set of statistical methods implemented in Python for galaxy classification and probabilistic inference from photometry.
  The methods use a reference sample with known galaxy properties. They rely on explicit, physically interpretable criteria and make no assumptions about the functional form of the color distributions. Binary selections are calibrated using either linear color conditions, particularly suited to background galaxy selection, or flexible color--color grids, seeking to maximize completeness given a threshold on purity. For probabilistic inference, the code identifies reference objects with compatible colors within photometric uncertainties to estimate each test galaxy's probability of satisfying user-defined criteria, as well as probability density functions of continuous properties such as redshift. Abstract: We present a new rescaling and stacking method to test cold dark matter predictions through the dynamical modelling of galaxy groups using the Jeans equation. Abstract: We study the production of primordial black holes (PBHs) and scalar-induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) in aligned axion inflation through the transition between two stages of inflation. The first stage proceeds along a metastable trajectory and ends when this trajectory loses stability, causing the fields to turn towards the attractor of the second inflationary stage. During the transition, coupled adiabatic--entropy evolution produces a localized enhancement of the curvature power spectrum. Abstract: We present a fast and accurate method for computing the two-loop matter power spectrum in the Effective Field Theory of Large-Scale Structure (EFTofLSS). Using FFTLog, we decompose the linear power spectrum into complex power laws, reducing the two-loop corrections to cosmology-independent massless Feynman integrals of kite topology. A central numerical challenge is the evaluation of these integrals for complex powers with large imaginary parts, where the highly oscillatory integrands make accurate numerical evaluation particularly challenging. We overcome this difficulty using a Mellin--Barnes representation summed over a uniform lattice, achieving approximately ten digit precision even for large imaginary powers, with an error estimate for each integral. The resulting precomputed matrices allow the two-loop power spectrum to be evaluated for a new cosmology in a few milliseconds. Comparing with direct numerical integration, we find agreement at the $10^{-4}$ level relative to the power spectrum up to $k=0.2$ $h$Mpc$^{-1}$ after accounting for EFTofLSS counterterm contributions and FFTLog decomposition errors. The same framework extends to biased tracers and redshift-space distortions, providing the building blocks for general two-loop calculations in EFTofLSS. We make the code for evaluating the kite integrals publicly available, together with precomputed matrices and an implementation for the two-loop matter power spectrum as a concrete application. Our results can be directly incorporated into existing FFTLog-based codes for large-scale structure, extending their current one-loop capabilities to two loops within the same computational framework.

</details>

Abstract: The technique of 21 cm intensity mapping (IM) is emerging as a possible, cost-effective method to supplement optical surveys to map the large-scale structure, and extend their redshift reach, with projects aiming to cover a large range of post-EoR redshifts, from z=0 to z=6. Since the early discussions 20 years ago, a few dedicated interferometers have been built, and surveys have been carried out using them as well as general purpose radio instruments. An introduction to late-time cosmology with 21 cm intensity mapping is given, highlighting the main scientific and technical challenges. We present then an overview of different instrument concepts and survey strategies, followed by a discussion of outstanding data analysis challenges, most notably the foreground subtraction, which is a major issue in IM. We carry on with a presentation of the currently active projects and the 21 cm signal measurements which have been obtained, either in cross-correlation with optical surveys, or as the autocorrelation power spectrum. Finally, we discuss briefly the prospects for this field with future instruments and surveys such as SKA, HIRAX and CHORD.

Abstract: In the context of stochastic inflation, specifying boundary conditions is often a requirement to distinguish domains where quantum diffusion takes place from regions where stochastic effects are suppressed compared to the standard slow-roll evolution. Yet, even though the transition between the two is expected to be smooth and continuous, most of the literature has hitherto focused on absorbing and reflective boundaries. We consider here the use of a broader class of boundaries, called elastic, which involves one free parameter interpolating between these two limiting cases. We show how the conventional stochastic-$δN$, and its recently proposed time-reversed counterpart adapt to such a class of boundary behaviour. As an illustration, we solve time-reversed stochastic inflation for an exactly flat semi-infinite potential with an elastic boundary, a setup where the standard stochastic-$δN$ formalism breaks down, and compare the results to the purely absorbing case. Quantitative differences are found in terms of skewness and tail behaviour. This suggests that the transition between stochastic inflation and subsequent evolution may have observable consequences. Finally, we show that an elastic boundary stands as an effective model for stochastic tunnelling through a Dirac delta potential barrier. This mechanism can be equivalently framed as a smooth transition of stochastic trajectories in a mirror virtual medium with specific absorption properties.

Abstract: Recent work has shown that a simple form of coupled dark energy with a constant coupling produces a good fit to the DESI data. In this scenario, dark matter, but not baryons, couples to a scalar field, thereby violating the weak equivalence principle (EP). We first generalize the coupled dark energy model by adding a nonminimal coupling between the scalar field and gravity, and dub the resulting model "doubly coupled dark energy". We then construct a parameter $E_P$ in this model that quantifies EP violation on cosmological scales and can be measured with large galaxy surveys in a model-independent way. Finally, we forecast constraints on the couplings for present and future spectroscopic galaxy surveys, namely DESI, Euclid, and SKA Phase 2 (SKA2). To keep the analysis as general as possible, we leave the initial power spectrum shape and the galaxy bias free to vary in several bins. Among these surveys, SKA2 could provide the tightest constraint on the coupling strength in the coupled dark energy scenario, with a relative $1σ$ uncertainty of $22\%$. The constraints weaken when both couplings are varied, but can be further improved by increasing the number of galaxies detected.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系本征对齐（IA）是弱引力透镜测量中重要的系统误差来源，同时也是星系形成与演化模型的重要检验手段；传统模型仅基于潮汐场建模IA，但星系邻近环境与质量等属性可能带来额外对齐效应。

Method: 采用IllustrisTNG宇宙学流体模拟与SDSS-III（BOSS）观测数据，开展模型无关的密度加权IA检验；利用星系形状、真实潮汐场及模拟盒背景潮汐场的两点相关函数，评估潮汐对齐范式。

Result: 密度加权主要增强大尺度IA振幅，小尺度影响依赖样本；中心星系在1-halo区仍与非线性潮汐场呈良好线性对齐，且该结果在测量精度范围内成立。

Conclusion: 密度加权是IA建模中不可忽略的因素，尤其在大尺度上；潮汐对齐范式在IllustrisTNG中对中心星系具有较广适用性，支持其作为IA物理建模的基础。

Abstract: Intrinsic alignments (IA) of galaxies are an important source of systematic uncertainty in weak gravitational lensing measurements and also serve as a test for models of galaxy formation and evolution. These galaxy alignments are usually modeled assuming that the IA are sourced by the tidal fields around the galaxies. However, galaxy properties, like their proximity to other galaxies and their mass, provide alignment contributions beyond the tidal field. In this work, we perform novel model-agnostic tests to determine the impact of density-weighting on the IA of galaxies using data from the IllustrisTNG cosmological hydrodynamical simulation and SDSS-III (BOSS). We show that the primary effect of density-weighting is an increase of the alignment amplitude at large scales, whereas the small-scale impact is sample-dependent. We also assess the tidal alignment paradigm for IllustrisTNG galaxies using two-point correlation functions of galaxy shapes, tidal fields, and the sampled background tidal field of the simulation box. We find, given the precision of our measurements, that central galaxies align linearly to the underlying (non-linear) tidal field well into the 1-halo regime.

Abstract: The present study delves into the gravitational lensing phenomena in the weak field limit of a charged black hole in the mimetic Euler-Heisenberg theory. Additionally, the Einstein ring radius is analytically derived as a direct implementation of the deflection angle. The relevant influence of the Euler-Heisenberg parameter and the charge parameter on the Einstein ring radius is briefly discussed. We also investigate the observational implications of lensing in the weak gravitational limit of the black hole. The magnification and distortion parameter are also analyzed in this manuscript. Furthermore, the time delay of light due to gravitational lensing is also briefly discussed. Our study reveals the significant influence of the model parameters of the specified black hole on the gravitational lensing signatures.

Abstract: Transparent magnetic wormholes can share a black hole exterior while their supporting matter controls the response of the throat. We construct symmetric thin shells from the magnetic black hole of exponential nonlinear electrodynamics and derive their equilibrium stresses and linear radial stability criterion. Restricting the seed to its zero-bare-mass magnetic branch ties the charge and coupling along the fixed-mass family. In sampled cases, calibrated linear barotropic shells are unstable, whereas variable phantom and variable Chaplygin laws admit locally stable radial sectors. Explicit radius dependence strengthens the phantom restoring term; the Chaplygin response cancels the divergent inverse lapse contribution and permits stable sectors close to a non-extremal seed horizon. The surface density remains negative, although an inner interval satisfies the tangential null and intrinsic strong energy conditions. At the seed photon sphere, the two variable laws share an exact stabilization threshold. The complete radial averaged null energy remains negative even when the proper shell energy tends to zero. Numerical null geodesic imaging uses independent, minimally coupled light and transparent junctions, with celestial sources and rotating thin disks truncated at the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO). For observer-side disk emission with the prescribed ISCO truncation and a throat below the seed photon sphere, inclined images coincide with the seed black hole image. A throat above that sphere removes selected returning contributions while preserving much of the bright primary structure. Illuminating the opposite exterior adds inner disk arcs, but a central dark region can survive because the disk does not illuminate every transmitted direction. Image degeneracy depends jointly on the retained null trajectories and the emitting support, rather than on the presence of a horizon alone.

Abstract: The angular winding of a returning light ray can grow while the family of admissible impacts disappears. We characterize this competition near reflection symmetry in a static thin-shell wormhole joining Schwarzschild exteriors of slightly different masses. The result remains valid when the throat distance divided by the mass contrast tends to zero, stays finite or diverges. For a throat distance proportional to a positive power of the fractional mass difference, the width exponent increases from one toward two and then saturates, while the logarithmic winding coefficient decreases from three toward two. Fixed-throat configurations retain their finite-distance amplitude and coefficient three. Reference-subtracted profiles connect the intermediate sector to finite and vanishing scaled throat positions. Independent quadratures test the scaling laws and resolve the slower convergence associated with changing scale ratios. The shrinking solid-angle aperture follows directly from the critical-impact gap. For an axial observer and a transparent test disk confined to the same exterior, the return-phase condition selects the admitted intersections; sampled near-symmetric configurations shift the first available global crossing to a later label. Abstract: Massive spin-2 fields on the Kerr black hole generally obey coupled partial differential equations. We show that these equations separate through two commuting angular operators acting on five components of the field, its curl and its linearised Weyl tensor. A joint angular eigenfunction fixes the relative profiles of the five polarisations and leaves one second-order radial equation. The separation constants satisfy an algebraic relation that follows from the angular operators themselves. The construction removes the obstruction to a Teukolsky-type equation by a radial transport identity, then eliminates the additional radial solution through a first integral. It provides ordinary differential equations for massive spin-2 fields on Kerr, and, equivalently, for Kaluza-Klein perturbations of the Kerr black string.

</details>

Abstract: First-order phase transitions in a dark sector can produce an observable stochastic gravitational-wave background, and many models predicting such signals in current and future experiments have been explored. For moderately strong transitions, hydrodynamical simulations suggest that sound waves generated by the expansion and collisions of true-vacuum bubbles are the dominant source of gravitational waves. It is often assumed that these sound waves propagate only within the dark-sector fluid, while the impact of the visible sector is neglected. In this paper, we investigate the effects of a weak coupling between the dark and visible fluids on the resulting gravitational-wave signal. We adopt a phenomenological hydrodynamical description of two ultra-relativistic fluids, parametrizing their interactions without specifying an underlying microscopic model. We focus on the regime in which the coupling can be neglected during the phase transition but becomes relevant during the subsequent, longer-lasting period of sound wave propagation. Within this framework, we analytically show that, even if the bubble expansion initially affects only the dark fluid, sound waves can subsequently be induced in the visible sector. Using the sound-shell model, we find that, despite the additional sound waves generated in the visible sector, the total gravitational-wave signal sourced by both fluids is generally suppressed compared to the case of a completely decoupled dark sector. This suppression becomes more pronounced for larger interaction rates and a larger ratio of relativistic degrees of freedom in the visible sector to those in the dark sector. The coupling also modifies the shape of the gravitational-wave spectrum.

</details>

Abstract: The problem of describing the dynamics of quantum systems relative to quantum clocks with which they interact is relevant to the physics of quantum reference frames and quantum gravity alike. There have been different approaches that derive a relational time-dependent Schrödinger equation for the system relative to the clock from a time-independent Hamiltonian constraint. We show that some of these approaches, due to Smith and Ahmadi, Gemsheim and Rost, as well as the so-called Born-Oppenheimer approach, can be unified in a single framework for describing conditional quantum states. In particular, the Born-Oppenheimer approach to quantum cosmology is seen to correspond to a type of interacting Page-Wootters formalism. We discuss the issue of unitarity and linearity, or lack thereof, in the relational dynamics, and we conclude with a brief outlook to further developments.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Black holes offer a unique setting to test gravity in the strong-field regime, where deviations from General Relativity are expected to be most pronounced. We study how modified gravity changes the structure of an advection-dominated accretion flow (ADAF) around a supermassive black hole. The gravitational field governing the disc is described by a Paczyński--Wiita-like potential derived in Eddington-inspired Born--Infeld (EiBI) gravity, formulated \textit{à la} Palatini. We solve the ADAF-like equations for this potential and compare the radial profiles of the flow with those obtained for the standard Paczyński--Wiita potential. The largest difference appears in the gas density, which is several orders of magnitude higher in the EiBI case near the inner region and converges to the standard result at large radii. The specific angular momentum and temperature are lower in EiBI gravity, while the radial velocity differs mainly at large radii for both solution branches, $ s=-1$ and $+1$. The angular velocity and the disk aspect ratio $H/r$ change only slightly. These results show that EiBI gravity can modify the density structure of hot accretion flows in the strong-field regime, potentially providing observational discriminators.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate bounds on the minimum orbital period around four-dimensional general static spherical black holes. For all static spherically symmetric black holes with an event horizon, asymptotic flatness and non-negative energy density outside the horizon, we prove $T_{\min}\leq 6\sqrt{3}\,πM$. The bound is sharp and is saturated by the Schwarzschild solution. The lower bound requires an additional condition on the exterior matter: under the tangential pressure condition $p_τ\geq(κ/2)ρ$ with $κ>1$, imposed for all $r\geq r_{g}$, where $r_{g}$ is the largest radius at which $T(r)$ attains its global minimum, we prove $T_{\min}\geq c(κ)M$ with $c(κ)=6\sqrt{3}π(κ-1)\sqrt{κ+2}/κ^{3/2}$. In summary, both bounds apply to the same geometric class and differ only in what they demand of the matter: non-negative energy density suffices for the upper bound, while the lower bound is established under the additional tangential pressure condition.

</details>

Abstract: We present an analytical study of the global and asymptotic properties of static, spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat solutions in general relativity and its \(D\)-dimensional generalization with multiple minimally coupled scalar fields described by a general nonlinear sigma model. Assuming a complete Riemannian target space and a non-negative scalar potential satisfying suitable growth conditions, we prove that solutions remain regular at every finite nonzero radius and can be extended inward to a naked singularity at the center. In particular, this excludes singularities on spherical shells at finite radius. We further show that all solutions exhibit universal asymptotic behavior near the central singularity. Finally, we demonstrate that these spacetimes can possess at most one photon sphere, which, if present, is necessarily unstable.

</details>

Conclusion: 端到端注入研究对准确评估偏心双星探测能力至关重要；仅靠波形匹配不足以反映真实搜寻性能；当前GstLAL流程对高偏心率信号具有一定鲁棒性，尤其对高质量系统。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from eccentric compact binary coalescences trace dynamical binary formation, but matched-filtering searches responsible for most detections rely on quasicircular templates. Quantifying any sensitivity loss is crucial for assessing population completeness and the need for dedicated eccentric template banks. We present the first systematic GstLAL search study spanning eccentricities up to $e_{\rm 15Hz}=0.99$ and broad ranges of chirp mass, mass ratio, inclination, and aligned spin. Using TEOBResumS-Dalí waveforms, we inject eccentric signals and measure recovered signal-to-noise ratio, signal-consistency statistics, false-alarm rate, and sensitive volume and distance. For high-mass binaries ($\mathcal{M}_c\gtrsim30\,M_\odot$), sensitive distance is nearly independent of eccentricity, demonstrating that greater eccentricity does not necessarily reduce search sensitivity. In contrast, for low-mass binaries, sensitive distance falls by up to $\sim50\%$ near $e_{\rm 15Hz}\approx0.75$. Although the absolute loss in reach is modest given their low quasicircular sensitivity, this relative reduction exceeds the increase in sensitive distance between the second and third observing runs of the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA collaboration and is consistent with previous low-mass studies. We also find that waveform matches at fixed source parameters substantially overpredict search sensitivity loss, meaning that the pipeline's signal-consistency test and maximization over its template bank mitigate much of the predicted sensitivity loss. This highlights the need for end-to-end injection campaigns to establish the detectability of eccentric binaries.

</details>


### [99] [Dynamics of spinning test particles in the innermost stable circular orbit of an approximate Kerr-like spacetime with mass quadrupole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41036)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自旋对强引力场中轨道动力学的影响，特别是修正标准Kerr时空下ISCO参数，以更精确描述致密天体周围自旋粒子的行为。

Method: 基于Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon框架，结合Euler-Lagrange形式与Hamilton动力学，运用有效势方法分析共转与反向旋转构型，并取Schwarzschild、Lense-Thirring和Kerr极限进行验证。

Result: 得到了含自旋与四极矩参数的通用ISCO方程，以及能量和角动量的解析表达式；在各经典极限下导出了适用于自旋粒子的新ISCO公式。

Conclusion: 粒子自旋显著影响ISCO半径、能量和角动量，该工作将传统无自旋ISCO理论推广至自旋测试粒子情形，为引力波建模与极端质量比旋进系统研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the motion of spinning test particles in a deformed, rotating spacetime described by a metric perturbed up to second order in the mass quadrupole moment. We examine how the parameters of the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) --namely the radius, energy, and angular momentum-- are modified by the particle's spin, contrasting our findings with both the spinless case and standard Kerr benchmarks. By employing effective potential techniques, we consider both co-rotating and counter-rotating configurations, corresponding to parallel and antiparallel alignments of the angular momenta. Using the Euler--Lagrange formalism and Hamiltonian dynamics within the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon framework, we derive an explicit general ISCO equation incorporating the spin and quadrupole parameters, alongside analytical expressions for the conserved orbital quantities. Furthermore, by taking the Schwarzschild, Lense--Thirring and Kerr limits within our generalized framework, we establish new ISCO equations and compact expressions for the energy and angular momentum that extend these fundamental spacetimes to the case of spinning test particles.

</details>


### [100] [Probing dark sector with decihertz gravitational wave observations of compact binary inspirals in IndIGO-D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41473)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在decihertz频段引力波观测中，利用致密双星旋近信号探测原子暗物质（aDM）及其通过超轻质量暗光子相互作用的可能性；提出了一个中子星积累带电暗物质并引入长程Yukawa相互作用的模型，推导出引力波波形中的特征修正项，并通过贝叶斯参数估计分析表明未来空间decihertz探测器（如IndIGO-D）可高精度测量暗光子质量（达10^{-20} eV量级），从而成为检验暗区物理的重要工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用decihertz频段引力波观测探测原子暗物质及其与超轻暗光子相互作用的新途径，弥补传统天体物理和粒子实验在该参数空间的灵敏度不足。

Method: 构建中子星积累带电暗物质、通过暗Abelian矢量中介子产生Yukawa相互作用的理论模型；导出含-1PN偶极辐射及有限中介子质量效应的修正引力波波形；将其纳入参数化广义相对论检验框架；对模拟的BNS和NSBH信号，在IndIGO-D探测器响应下开展贝叶斯参数估计分析。

Result: 对于类似GW170817的BNS系统，若aDM耦合接近当前观测上限，暗光子静止能量可被以90%置信度精度达O(10^{-20}) eV测定；当耦合较弱（α≲10^{-8}）时，仅当偶极辐射贡献显著时仍可约束中介子质量；Yukawa耦合与偶极辐射贡献共同决定参数可测性。

Conclusion: 未来decihertz引力波天文台（如IndIGO-D）有望成为高精度探测暗区物理、特别是致密双星中暗物质相互作用的强有力实验室。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of decihertz gravitational-wave (GW) observations of compact-binary inspirals to probe atomic dark matter (aDM) and its interactions through an ultralight massive dark photon. We consider a scenario in which neutron stars accumulate DM charged under a dark Abelian massive vector mediator, leading to an additional long-range Yukawa interaction between compact objects and associated dipolar radiation. In this framework, the GW waveform acquires characteristic corrections parameterized by the Yukawa coupling strength $α$, the dipole-radiation parameter $γ$, and a parameter related to the mediator mass $μ$. These corrections include a leading $-1$ post-Newtonian dipole contribution and finite-mediator-mass effects. We relate the aDM corrections to existing parameterized tests of general relativity and perform Bayesian parameter-estimation analyses for simulated binary neutron star (BNS) and neutron star-black hole (NSBH) systems incorporating the modified binary inspiral in a proposed space-based decihertz detector, named IndIGO-D. For a GW170817-like BNS with nonzero aDM couplings chosen near current observational bounds, we find that the dark-photon rest energy can be inferred with a $90\%$ credible uncertainty of $O(10^{-20})\,\mathrm{eV}$, conditional on a detectable dark-sector contribution. Analyses with weaker dark-sector interactions $(α\lesssim O(10^{-8}))$ show that the mediator mass remains unconstrained when both the Yukawa coupling and dipole-radiation contribution are negligible, but can be measured even for a negligible Yukawa coupling if the dipole-radiation contribution is sufficiently strong. Our results demonstrate that future decihertz GW observatories can serve as powerful laboratories for testing dark-sector physics and probing dark matter interactions in compact binaries through precision GW measurements.

</details>


### [101] [Probing Realistic Neutron Star Equations of State via the Chameleon Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41505)
*Debadri Bhattacharjee,Grigorios Panotopoulos,Pradip Kumar Chattopadhyay*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究变色龙机制（一种修改引力理论）如何影响中子星这类致密天体的宏观结构，并结合观测约束限定其理论参数。

Method: 基于真实的核子状态方程，数值求解广义相对论框架下引入变色龙标量场后的恒星结构方程（即Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程的推广形式），分析不同标量-物质耦合参数β对中子星质量-半径关系的影响。

Result: 变色龙机制显著改变中子星最大质量和半径；现有高质量和高精度的中子星质量与半径观测数据（如NICER、脉冲星计时）对耦合参数β施加了严格上限。

Conclusion: 变色龙机制虽在弱场下可被屏蔽，但在中子星强引力环境中仍可观测其效应；中子星是检验此类修改引力理论的重要天体实验室。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of the Chameleon mechanism on structural properties of neutron stars adopting realistic nucleonic equations of state. The scalar-matter coupling parameter $β$ controls the departure from the corresponding general-relativistic configurations, affecting both the maximum mass and the stellar radius. Our numerical results demonstrate that the chameleon mechanism can have a significant impact on the macroscopic properties of neutron stars, even when realistic equations of state are employed. Moreover, well established constraints on masses and radii of neutron stars put stringent limits on the allowed numerical values of the coupling.

</details>


### [102] [Novel thermodynamic topological subclass from inverse-temperature asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41585)
*Dejiang Yin,Qi-Qi Liang,Yu-Die Wan,Li-yun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电McGuigan--Nappi--Yost黑洞在固定电荷正则系综下的热力学拓扑结构，发现其热力学缺陷结构属于新提出的子类$\breve W^{1+}$，特征是逆霍金温度在大视界极限下趋于有限正值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别黑洞热力学相结构中的新拓扑类别，拓展现有热力学拓扑分类框架。

Method: 基于广义离壳自由能构建热力学矢量场，分析其零点及逆霍金温度的渐近行为，结合拓扑 winding 数刻画热力学缺陷结构。

Result: 发现该黑洞仅存在一个局部稳定的热力学分支，其拓扑数为 $w=+1$，但因逆温度在大视界极限下趋于有限正值，不属于已知 $W^{1+}$ 类，而构成新子类 $\breve W^{1+}$。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了黑洞热力学中由不同渐近行为导致的新拓扑子类，表明其他黑洞族可能蕴含更多未被发现的热力学拓扑结构。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic topology of the charged McGuigan--Nappi--Yost black hole in the canonical ensemble at fixed electric charge, identifying equilibrium black hole solutions as zeros of the thermodynamic vector field constructed from the generalized off-shell free energy. The inverse Hawking temperature diverges as the extremal limit is approached and approaches a finite positive value in the large-horizon limit. The zero points of the thermodynamic vector field reveal a thermodynamic phase structure consisting solely of a locally stable branch with positive winding number ($w=+1$). The innermost small black hole state is locally stable in the low-temperature limit. As the temperature increases, the stable branch extends toward the outermost large black hole regime, while no black hole states exist in the high-temperature regime beyond the finite upper Hawking temperature. This distinct asymptotic behavior of the inverse temperature places the thermodynamic defect structure outside the established topological (sub)classes, although its topological number agrees with that of the standard $W^{1+}$ class. We therefore identify this defect structure as a new thermodynamic topological subclass, $\breve W^{1+}$. This finding extends the existing classification to include a finite, nonzero large-horizon limit of the inverse Hawking temperature and suggests that distinct asymptotic behaviors in other black hole families may reveal further topological (sub)classes and thermodynamic phase structures.

</details>


### [103] [Homogeneous Spectral Freedom and Hadamard Consistency of Perturbative Green Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41777)
*Bekir Can Lütfüoğlu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种判别方法，用于区分扰动模式方程中不同特解所表现出的短程奇异性是源于表征自由度还是量子态的真实改变；通过在两种脉冲平面波背景下的应用，验证了该判别法的有效性，并揭示了奇异性来源与齐次解及几何结构的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扰动模式方程的不同特解在谱求和时可能表现出不同的短距奇异性，但这些差异未必反映物理态的真实变化，亟需一种通用判据来区分表征自由度与真实量子态改变。

Method: 基于固定正常双曲算子下Hadamard双点函数之差必为光滑齐次双解这一性质，构建诊断准则；并应用于两类脉冲平面波时空，从度规出发严格重算波算子、模式函数、世界函数与Van Vleck行列式，结合精确模式匹配与几何构造进行比对。

Result: 在第一类可解平面波中，标准平直归一化方案给出的重整化应力张量在脉冲面外为零；在第二类中，修正度规使主导扰动由二次降至一次，消除了横向动量的反幂奇点，且仍保持精确的对齐零辐射爱因斯坦源；两类情形下奇异Green函数均精确对应Hadamard参数展开，额外对数项源于动量奇异的齐次数据。

Conclusion: 扰动级数中出现的非光滑项若非来自算子本身的改变，便必源于不合法的齐次谱数据；该判据统一解释了多种平面波背景下传播子奇异性结构的起源，并表明光滑前缘正则化可在不引入反向动量奇点的前提下复现匹配后的奇异结构。

Abstract: Perturbative mode equations often admit formally different particular solutions whose spectral sums appear to have different short-distance singularities. We formulate a general diagnostic which separates this representational freedom from a genuine change of quantum state: for a fixed normally hyperbolic operator, two Hadamard two-point functions can differ only by a smooth homogeneous bisolution. Thus, a nonsmooth term generated by rearranging a perturbative source must be traced either to a changed operator or to inadmissible homogeneous spectral data. We apply the criterion to two impulsive plane-wave backgrounds. Starting directly from the metrics, we recompute the wave operators, modes, world functions, and Van Vleck determinants. Exact-mode matching and the geometrical construction agree through second order for the first profile. In the second, correcting the metric changes the leading perturbation from quadratic to linear order in the wave parameter and eliminates inverse transverse-momentum poles, while the corrected metric retains an exact aligned null-radiation Einstein source. In both examples the singular Green function is precisely the perturbative expansion of the Hadamard parametrix; the additional logarithms arise from momentum-singular homogeneous data. For the exactly soluble first profile, the standard flat-normalized plane-wave prescription gives a vanishing renormalized stress tensor away from the impulsive surface. Smooth-front regularizations of both profiles reproduce, at the calculated orders and away from the characteristic zero mode, the singular structure of the matched impulsive propagators without generating inverse transverse-momentum poles.

</details>


### [104] [From thermogravity to thermocosmology with a running non-conservation scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41780)
*Emmanuel N. Saridakis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于热力学的宇宙学新框架——'热宇宙学'（thermocosmology），通过引入随哈勃参数变化的冷暗物质非守恒尺度L₂(H)，构建了两种非守恒模型；它们可导出具有类精质（quintessence）或类幻影（phantom）行为的有效几何暗能量，且能自然穿越幻影边界；动力学分析揭示了丰富的吸引子结构；观测拟合显示对正向当前物质非守恒有轻微偏好，信息准则在ΛCDM与热引力模型间呈微弱权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ΛCDM模型中暗能量本质不明，而热引力理论为宇宙加速提供热力学起源；本文旨在拓展该框架，使物质非守恒具有动力学尺度（而非固定常数），并限制于冷暗物质，以构造更自然、可观测检验的暗能量替代模型。

Method: 构建两类依赖哈勃参量H的冷暗物质非守恒模型：(1) 非守恒率正比于H（Hubble-proportional）；(2) 非守恒率反比于H（inverse-Hubble）；推导其弗里德曼方程与连续性方程，重构等效几何暗能量状态方程；开展相空间动力学系统分析，识别临界点及其稳定性；利用Pantheon+超新星、宇宙钟（CC）和DESI DR2 BAO数据进行贝叶斯参数约束与模型比较（AIC/BIC）。

Result: Hubble-proportional模型存在解析解，对应二次运行真空模型；inverse-Hubble模型在早期趋近标准物质稀释，并允许能量流方向反转；两类模型均能产生平滑穿越w = −1的暗能量演化；相空间中存在连续族的横向吸引de Sitter解；数据拟合显示当前物质非守恒率Γ₀ > 0有轻微统计支持（≈1.5σ）；AIC略偏爱热引力模型，BIC则支持ΛCDM。

Conclusion: 运行热宇宙学是一个自洽、可检验的替代理论框架，无需引入额外标量场或超加速机制，即可实现丰富而合理的暗能量动力学；其与当前高精度观测兼容，为理解宇宙加速提供了热力学新视角。

Abstract: Thermogravity offers a novel approach to cosmic acceleration, deriving trace-free gravitational equations and matter non-conservation from thermodynamic processes in spacetime. We extend this framework towards thermocosmology by introducing a running non-conservation scale $L_2(H)$, while restricting non-conservation to cold dark matter. We investigate two prescriptions, namely a Hubble-proportional rate and an inverse-Hubble running model, deriving their cosmological dynamics and effective geometric dark-energy description. The first admits an exact analytical solution and is related to quadratic running-vacuum cosmologies, while the second approaches standard matter dilution at early times and allows reversals in the direction of energy exchange. Interestingly, the reconstructed geometric dark energy can exhibit quintessence-like and phantom-like behavior, including smooth crossings of the phantom divide, without introducing a phantom field or superacceleration. A unified dynamical-system analysis reveals distinct matter-era critical configurations and a continuous family of de Sitter solutions that are transversely attracting under suitable conditions. Confronting both models with Pantheon+ Type Ia supernovae, cosmic chronometers, and DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations, we find a mild preference for positive present-day matter non-conservation. The Akaike information criterion slightly favors the thermogravity scenarios, whereas the Bayesian information criterion favors the simpler $Λ$CDM model. Thus, running thermocosmology provides a viable framework for nontrivial effective dark-energy dynamics.

</details>


### [105] [Finite-Depth Null Capture and Mass-History Sensitivity in Vaidya Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41922)
*Jorge Luis Palamara,Jorge R. Palamara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对动态黑洞时空中的过去指向类光射线的有限深度端点映射方法，并研究了其在Schwarzschild和Vaidya时空中的行为，揭示了有限深度临界角对质量历史的敏感性及截断收敛性的物理本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解动态黑洞中光子球附近光线行为对观测（如事件视界望远镜）至关重要；标准临界圆概念在非静态时空中不直接适用，需构建具物理可测性的有限深度刻画。

Method: 构造带光学深度截断的端点映射，结合正则性、横截性与分离性假设分析决策集性质；在Schwarzschild与Vaidya（特别是ingoing Vaidya）时空中显式计算有限深度捕获圆/临界角；利用后向伴随法推导一阶响应因果核，并通过非线性解与Richardson外推验证高阶项。

Result: Schwarzschild中有限深度捕获圆以指数速率exp[−L/(3M)]收敛至经典临界圆；两个局部度规完全相同的C⁴吸积剖面导致不同的有限深度临界角；得到一阶临界角偏移Δ_hist α_45的一阶与二阶ε展开式，并获得宽谱、非局域的因果响应核。

Conclusion: 有限深度临界结构不仅受截断引起的数值不稳定性影响，更本质地编码了全局质量演化历史信息；该敏感性独立于瞬时质量，挑战了仅用局部几何描述强引力透镜的简化范式。

Abstract: We formulate a finite-depth endpoint map for past-directed null rays in dynamical black-hole spacetimes, retaining an unresolved label when no terminal is reached before a prescribed optical depth. For regular transverse terminal hits, common-flow, transversality, and separation hypotheses make the regular decision sets open and the endpoint label locally constant. In Schwarzschild spacetime, the resulting finite-depth capture circle converges to the standard critical circle as $\exp[-L/(3M)]$ when the cutoff is measured in static-observer optical depth. We then apply the construction to ingoing Vaidya spacetime. Two $C^4$ accretion profiles that coincide on a neighborhood of the observation event, and therefore have identical local mass and metric jets there, nevertheless produce distinct finite-depth critical angles. For the baseline protocol, $Δ_{\mathrm{hist}}α_{45}=0.0819062ε-0.0231767ε^2+O(ε^3)$. A backward adjoint gives an explicit causal kernel representing the first-order response to the mass history. The kernel is broad, peaks away from the photon-sphere dwell region, and changes between finite backgrounds even where their instantaneous masses agree. The quadratic coefficient is independently confirmed by centered nonlinear solutions and Richardson extrapolation. These results separate instability-controlled cutoff convergence from globally transported mass-history sensitivity.

</details>


### [106] [Neutrino Oscillation in a Static Dihole Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41960)
*Rogério Capobianco,Rafael C. R. de Lima*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在静态等质量Majumdar-Papapetrou双黑洞几何中中微子味振荡行为，重点分析了对称面上的传播特性及不同时空构型（如双光环、临界合并光环、无光环）下引力透镜效应对中微子振荡概率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义相对论框架下强引力场（特别是多黑洞系统）对量子粒子（中微子）相干演化的影响，检验引力与量子效应的耦合机制。

Method: 在零近似下推导中微子相位主项，数值计算测地线积分；针对两味中微子模型，分析不同轨迹干涉对振荡概率的影响，并考察多种时空构型（含光环数目变化）下的过渡概率行为。

Result: 发现弱透镜区振荡概率随黑洞质量增大而增大，半分离变化影响不显著；强透镜区质量和半分离均显著调制概率极值的位置和高度；在临界和无光环构型中识别出三条零缠绕轨迹（含一条近对称轴路径），且振荡模式强烈依赖中微子能量。

Conclusion: 中微子振荡可作为探测多黑洞时空精细结构（如光环存在性与分布）的潜在探针，其振荡特征对时空参数和中微子质量序敏感，为引力与粒子物理交叉研究提供新途径。

Abstract: We investigate neutrino flavor oscillations in the static equal-mass Majumdar-Papapetrou dihole geometry, restricting the propagation to the symmetry plane. Within the null approximation, we derive the leading contribution to the neutrino phase and numerically evaluate the geodesic integrals. For two neutrino flavors, interference between different trajectories introduces a dependence on the individual neutrino masses and on the neutrino mass ordering for nonmaximal mixing. We analyze the transition probability in different spacetime configurations possessing two light rings, a merged light ring (critical configuration), and no light rings in the equatorial plane. In the weak-lensing regime, the transition probability generally increases with the black holes' individual masses, while variations in the half-separation produce no qualitative change in the transition probability. In stronger-lensing configurations, variations in both the mass and half-separation modify the heights and positions of the extrema, with no monotonic increase or decrease in the transition probability. For the critical and no-light-ring configurations studied, we identify three zero-winding trajectories connecting the source and detector, including one passing near the symmetry axis. The probability remains oscillatory, and its oscillatory pattern depends strongly on the neutrino energy.

</details>


### [107] [Scattering of massless scalar waves by higher-dimensional regular black holes in general relativity coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41972)
*Bobir Toshmatov,Askar Abdikamalov,Sardor Tojiev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维正则黑洞对无质量标量波的散射，结合经典测地线分析、广义D维glory近似和部分波分析，揭示了散射截面在前向方向的发散行为及电荷参数与维度对glory条纹的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维非线性电动力学耦合下正则黑洞（统一Bardeen和Hayward模型）对标量波的散射特性，特别是几何光学极限下不稳定类光圆轨道的作用。

Method: 结合经典类光测地线分析、推广至D维的glory近似方法，以及在D=5,6,7维下的部分波分析。

Result: 固定频率下，散射截面在前向θ→0时以θ^{-4}发散，系数由质量决定；更陡峭的经典律θ^{-(D-2)^2/(D-3)}仅在较大角度和更高频率出现；电荷减小临界与glory冲击参数、展宽glory条纹；高维下glory条纹更少更宽、后向强度更弱；匹配构型中后向强度和低频吸收差异最显著。

Conclusion: 高维正则黑洞的波散射特征强烈依赖于维度和电荷参数，前向散射普适性强，而glory效应和吸收谱可作为区分不同模型的关键观测指标。

Abstract: We study the scattering of planar massless scalar waves by the family of higher-dimensional regular black holes in general relativity coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics, which unifies the Bardeen and Hayward models in any dimension. The scalar field is a neutral test field that propagates in the bulk, and in the geometric-optics limit, its rays are the null geodesics of the spacetime, so that the unstable circular null geodesics of the background geometry control the scattering. We combine the classical scattering of null geodesics, the glory approximation, which we generalize to $D$ dimensions, and the partial-wave analysis in $D=5,6,7$. We show that at a fixed frequency, the wave cross section diverges in the forward direction as $θ^{-4}$ in any dimension, with a coefficient fixed by the mass, while the steeper classical law $θ^{-(D-2)^{2}/(D-3)}$ appears only at larger angles and higher frequencies. The charge parameter decreases the critical and the glory impact parameters and widens the glory fringes, and in higher dimensions, the glory pattern has fewer and wider fringes and a weaker backward intensity. For the matched configurations studied here, backward intensity and low-frequency absorption provide the clearest differences, while the intermediate-to-high-frequency spectra are close.

</details>


### [108] [A Charged Compact Star Solution for a Conducting Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42051)
*Kai Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带电恒星模型，将均匀密度恒星视为导电球体，电荷均匀分布于表面；得到了背景时空度规的解析解，并发现内部流体压力在表面附近为负（向内），但当电荷过大时中心压力又变为正（向外）；同时通过有限差分法研究了轴向微扰，发现电磁与引力轴向扰动耦合，且拟正则模波形随电荷变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究电荷对恒星结构与稳定性的影响，特别是电荷引起的排斥力与电磁场引力效应之间的平衡关系，以及带电时空中的扰动行为。

Method: 将恒星建模为表面带电的均匀密度导电球体，用δ函数描述表面电荷分布；解析求解爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程得到背景度规；采用有限差分法数值研究轴向引力与电磁耦合扰动。

Result: 获得带电恒星背景度规的解析解；发现内部压力符号随电荷大小发生反转；揭示轴向电磁与引力扰动在星体内不可分离、相互耦合；拟正则模波形随电荷变化。

Conclusion: 电荷显著影响恒星内部力学平衡与时空扰动特性：小电荷下静电力主导需负压平衡，大电荷下电磁场引力主导需正压平衡；轴向扰动的耦合性表明带电恒星的稳定性分析必须联合处理引力与电磁自由度。

Abstract: This paper considers the simplest charged stellar model, namely, a uniform density star with electric charge distributed on its surface. In this model, we treat the uniform density star as a conducting sphere, where all electric charges are uniformly distributed on the surface and represented by a delta function. We obtain an analytical solution for the background metric of the star spacetime and find that the pressure of the perfect fluid inside the star is directed inward (negative pressure) near the inner surface. This is because the repulsive force between surface electric charges requires an inward pressure to maintain equilibrium. However, when the charge becomes too large, the pressure of the ideal fluid at the stellar center gets directed outwards again. This is because the electric field itself is also a part of the stress energy tensor $T_{μν}$, acting as a source of gravity. When the gravitational effect of the electric field dominates, the perfect fluid must provide outward pressure to balance the inward gravitational attraction. On the other hand, we have also studied the axial perturbation in charged spacetimes using the finite difference method. We found that axial Maxwell perturbation and axial gravitational perturbation are coupled in the star spacetime, making it difficult to separate the variables by coordinate transformation. The computational results show that the waveforms of quasinormal modes vary with different charges.

</details>


### [109] [Hyperbolic scaling of the small-exponent limit in magnetic nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42091)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,R. Martínez von Dossow,Edson Otoniel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了分数幂非线性电动力学中磁时空在参数极限下趋近于Schwarzschild时空但仍保持电磁奇异性的问题，揭示了质量与磁荷贡献的分离、高阶轨道与散射修正，以及非线性本构响应如何在近真空背景下定义独特的光学极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在参数极限下，磁时空虽几何上趋近Schwarzschild解，但其电磁特性仍保持奇异性这一反直觉现象，理解非线性电动力学中几何与场响应的解耦机制。

Method: 采用渐近分析方法，固定渐近质量、磁荷和非线性耦合常数，令非线性指数趋于零；推导精确外部电磁能量表达式，计算轨道、光线偏折与散射函数的二阶修正；对比不同质量约束（如等于电磁自能）下的收敛行为。

Result: 发现存在一个随指数逆四次方增长的径向边界，其外才有共同物理时间方向；获得完整的二阶轨道与散射修正，分离质量与电荷贡献；确定弱场下本构弯曲与有限质量尾项的竞争尺度；给出强偏折对数系数及两阶近似的适用范围与精度。

Conclusion: 非线性本构响应可在近乎真空的背景中支撑一个独立的外部光学极限，该极限不依赖于传统几何主导图像，而由电磁本构性质主导；分析验证了多参数展开的一致性与收敛性。

Abstract: A magnetic spacetime can approach Schwarzschild while its electromagnetic characteristics remain singular in the same parameter limit. We study this separation in fractional-power nonlinear electrodynamics as the exponent tends to zero with asymptotic mass, magnetic charge and nonlinear coupling independently fixed. The extraordinary polarization admits a common physical time direction only outside a boundary that grows as the inverse fourth root of the exponent. On this radial scale, the lapse tends to unity but the constitutive angular response retains a nondegenerate barrier and finite scaled critical data. An exact representation of the exterior electromagnetic energy yields complete second-order corrections to the orbit and impact, separating mass and charge contributions and their distinct cancellations. Constraining the mass to equal the electromagnetic self-energy preserves the leading critical constants while changing the approach from fourth-root to square-root corrections. Returning rays remain inside the admissible exterior and define a limiting scattering function with a calculated logarithmic strong-deflection coefficient. In the weak regime, constitutive bending decays as the inverse fourth power of scaled impact, whereas finite mass supplies a slower inverse-impact tail. We determine their competition scale, its accessibility above the critical impact, and the accuracy of the two-term approximation. Independent energy, orbit and scattering calculations check the expansions and resolve their parameter-dependent convergence. The analysis shows how a nearly vacuum background can support a distinct exterior optical limit through the nonlinear constitutive response.

</details>


### [110] [Amplification of metric perturbations by extremal horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23878)
*Samuel E. Gralla,Sepehr Salamat*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发现，在极端Kerr-Newman黑洞中，当外部存在与视界共旋转的持续源时，视界附近的共旋转扰动会被显著增强，导致下落观测者经历异常大的潮汐形变；该效应源于极端极限下的离散自相似性及复数标度指数，并构成一种受驱的Aretakis不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究极端Kerr-Newman黑洞中下落观测者在穿越视界时是否可能感受到非平凡物理效应，挑战‘无毛定理’下通常认为的平滑穿越图像。

Method: 基于广义相对论中引力-电磁耦合扰动（GEM）理论，结合近似共旋转模式和数值求解，分析极端KN时空中的标度行为与复数标度指数；引入离散自相似性框架解释增强机制，并类比黑洞Meissner效应。

Result: 发现当电荷Q < Q_* ≈ 0.93M时，存在复数标度指数−1/2 ± iα，导致共旋转扰动在视界附近发散增强；该指数同时预言了GEM扰动在视界上/下的Aretakis型增长与衰减行为。

Conclusion: 极端KN黑洞中存在一种受外部共旋转源驱动的视界扰动增强现象，本质上是离散自相似性引发的‘驱动型Aretakis不稳定性’，表明极端黑洞视界具有非平凡的动力学响应特性。

Abstract: Under normal circumstances, an observer falling into a black hole feels nothing special upon crossing the horizon. In this paper we find an intriguing exception: For most of the parameter range of the extremal Kerr-Newman (KN) spacetime, horizon co-rotating perturbations are enhanced near the horizon, such that an infalling observer experiences an anomalously large tidal deformation as they enter the black hole. Such perturbations arise when there is a persistent source outside the black hole that is either co-rotating itself or has discrete Fourier support at the associated frequencies $ω=mΩ_H$ (where $m$ is the azimuthal number and $Ω_H$ is the horizon frequency). The enhancement is formally infinite at precise co-rotation, and we work with a nearly co-rotating mode to keep perturbation theory under control. The underlying physics is the emergence of discrete self-similarity in the extremal limit, with complex scaling weights of the form $-1/2\pm iα$ for real $α$. It is analogous to the black hole Meissner effect, except that the near-horizon field is enhanced rather than screened. We numerically calculate the scaling exponents for coupled gravitoelectromagnetic (GEM) perturbations of extremal KN black holes and show that the complex exponents arise in the parameter range $Q < Q_*$ with $Q_*\approx.93M$. These exponents also predict the decay and growth rates (Aretakis effect) of generic GEM perturbations of the KN spacetime, both on and off the horizon. The enhancement of co-rotating perturbations can be viewed as a driven Aretakis instability.

</details>


### [111] [Teleparallel Renormalization of Gravitational Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31444)
*Martin Krššák,Michal Stano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论的挠率等价理论（teleparallel equivalent of general relativity）中引力作用量，指出自旋联络通过边界项贡献于作用量，并可作为有效抵消项实现正则化；以Schwarzschild时空为例澄清若干歧义，证明拟局域作用量与广义相对论标准结果一致，并确立该正则化方案在适当挠率衰减条件下具有唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决挠率引力框架下引力作用量定义中的歧义问题，特别是自旋连接的作用、不同标架选择的影响以及体作用量与拟局域作用量之间的表面不一致性。

Method: 基于挠率等价广义相对论框架，分析自旋连接通过边界项对引力作用量的贡献；以Schwarzschild时空为具体例子，比较不同标架（如对角帧与规范帧）下的欧氏与洛伦兹作用量；考察奇点相关贡献及挠率张量渐进行为对正则化的约束。

Result: 证实拟局域作用量与广义相对论结果一致；发现恰当选取的自旋连接起有效抵消项作用；对角帧和规范帧给出相同欧氏作用量，但仅对角帧复现Wheeler–DeWitt区域上的正确洛伦兹作用量；在适当挠率衰减条件下，正则化方案唯一确定欧氏作用量。

Conclusion: 挠率引力中自旋连接的合理选取不仅可消除作用量发散，还能统一不同计算路径的结果；该正则化方法具有物理基础与数学唯一性，强化了挠率引力作为广义相对论替代表述的自洽性。

Abstract: We study the gravitational action in the teleparallel equivalent of general relativity, where the spin connection contributes to the action through a surface term and, when chosen appropriately, regularizes the action by effectively playing the role of a counterterm. We resolve several apparent ambiguities in this procedure using the Schwarzschild spacetime as an example. We show that the apparent discrepancy between bulk and quasilocal actions originates from contributions associated with spacetime singularities, and that the quasilocal action agrees with the standard results obtained in general relativity. We further clarify the role of the tetrad choice in determining the corresponding spin connection, showing that the proper diagonal and canonical frames give the same Euclidean action, while only the proper diagonal frame reproduces the expected Lorentzian action on the Wheeler--DeWitt patch. Finally, we establish the uniqueness of the regularization scheme and show that imposing an appropriate fall-off behavior of the torsion tensor removes the remaining ambiguity and fixes the Euclidean action uniquely.

</details>
