### [112] [Agentic Systems for Calibrating Cosmological Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40685)
*Chaipat Tirapongprasert,Matthew Ho*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticTuner，一种基于大语言模型的智能体校准流程，用于高效优化宇宙学模拟参数，在显著降低CPU成本的同时达到有竞争力的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯优化方法将模拟器视为黑箱，无法融入人类领域知识和工作流，限制了校准效率与可解释性。

Method: 设计AgenticTuner框架，利用现成大语言模型作为智能体优化器，依据非形式化的人类可读先验（如代码、文档、启发式指南）自主提出下一轮参数试验。

Result: 在宇宙学模拟校准任务中，AgenticTuner以远低于传统方法的CPU开销实现了具有竞争力的校准性能。

Conclusion: 大语言模型作为科学优化智能体具备潜力，为构建能执行复杂、长周期科学任务的AI科学家提供了初步范例。

Abstract: Calibrating a cosmological simulation is an expensive, high-dimensional parameter optimization task. Previous works employ Bayesian optimization for automation, which treats the simulator as a black box and precludes the incorporation of human domain knowledge or workflow into the selection policy. We propose AgenticTuner, an agentic calibration pipeline with a present-day large language model serving as the optimizer who proposes the next parameter trial. We show that these agents can achieve competitive calibration performance at a fraction of the CPU cost when they are given informal, human-readable priors (such as codebase, documentation, and heuristic guides that seasoned scientists may possess). We hope this work serves as an early step toward AI scientists that can carry out complex, long-horizon scientific tasks.

</details>


### [113] [Machine Learning Sensor Fusion for Water Vapor Radiometry based Tropospheric Delay Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41052)
*T. K. Sridharan,Yoshiharu Asaki,Brian E. Svoboda*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的水汽辐射计（WVR）延迟反演新方法，用于下一代甚大阵列（ngVLA）的对流层相位校正，通过异构多速率传感器融合框架，结合大气再分析数据、地面气象数据与天线级WVR谱，并利用卡尔曼滤波估计额外路径延迟，其参数由高采样率校准源相位学习得到；该方法正在开发中，计划首先在ALMA的183 GHz WVR系统上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前水汽辐射计的延迟反演方法无法满足ngVLA在高频段所需的精度和可靠性，亟需更优的相位校正技术。

Method: 提出一种基于机器学习的异构、多速率传感器融合架构：以大气再分析廓线和地面气象数据为背景，结合每副天线的22 GHz WVR谱，采用卡尔曼滤波估计大气额外路径延迟，其动态模型参数由高时间分辨率校准源相位数据训练学习。

Result: 构建了初步的机器学习驱动的WVR延迟反演框架，尚未给出定量实验结果，但已规划在ALMA 183 GHz WVR系统上开展首次验证。

Conclusion: 该方法有望显著提升ngVLA高频率观测中的相位稳定性，为未来射电干涉阵列的实时大气校正提供新范式。

Abstract: The next-generation Very Large Array (ngVLA) intends to use 22 GHz water vapor radiometry (WVR) to correct for tropospheric phase fluctuations in its high-frequency bands. However, current delay-retrieval methods do not reach the required accuracy and reliability. As a remedy, we propose a machine-learning based retrieval concept and a preliminary architecture, framed as heterogeneous, multi-rate sensor fusion. Atmospheric reanalysis profiles and surface weather set the context in which per-antenna WVR spectra are used to estimate excess path by a Kalman filter whose parameters are learned from high-cadence calibrator phases. The method is being developed, with planned first validation on the 183 GHz WVR system of the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA).

</details>


### [114] [An Assessment of Water Vapor Radiometry Landscape in ngVLA Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41071)
*T. K. Sridharan*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文讨论了下一代超大阵列（ngVLA）计划采用水汽辐射计（WVR）校正高频波段大气相位扰动，以降低校准开销；总结了当前紧凑型水汽辐射计（CWVR）在JVLA/ngVLA测试中失效的问题，分析其与ngVLA需求的差距，并提出实验室和实测验证路径，以及替代/补充方案（如利用VLA K波段自相关数据估计时延）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ngVLA需在高频波段（30.5–116 GHz）实现低于快切参考增益校准极限的大气相位校准精度，WVR被视为关键技术路径；但现有CWVR系统在VLA天线上未能有效跟踪可见度相位，亟需厘清原因并推进可行方案。

Method: 综述现有WVR经验与CWVR现状；分析CWVR性能缺陷与ngVLA需求之间的差距；提出分阶段验证路径：实验室测试（功能、稳定性、灵敏度、开关功率校准同步性）→天线实测→天空实测；并平行评估VLA K波段接收机自相关输出用于时延估计的可行性；探讨超越传统超额路径长估计的新方法。

Result: CWVR目前在VLA天线上无法跟踪可见度相位，缺乏统计显著的WVR校正实测数据支撑ngVLA性能预估；尚未确认其是否满足ngVLA对灵敏度、稳定性和同步性的严苛要求；K波段自相关等替代方法尚处评估阶段。

Conclusion: CWVR系统尚不能作为ngVLA WVR的即用解决方案，需系统性开展实验室与实测验证；应同步探索多种技术路径（包括非传统方法），以确保最终满足ngVLA对高精度、低开销大气校准的需求。

Abstract: The ngVLA intends to utilize Water Vapor Radiometry (WVR) as an approach to lower calibration overheads for tropospheric phase fluctuation correction in its high frequency bands (5 \& 6; 30.5-116 GHz) to levels below those feasible with fast switched reference gain calibration. As a stepping stone to an eventual ngVLA WVR system, the JVLA/ngVLA teams pursued an effort to develop and install a set of MMIC based compact water vapor radiometers (CWVR) and carry out on-sky testing. Most recent tests found that the CWVRs installed on the VLA antennas under this effort were non-functional in tracking visibility phases. As part of the program to develop a WVR system for the ngVLA, this memo summarizes the current landscape of water vapor radiometry experience, the situation with the CWVRs, how they relate to the ngVLA WVR requirements, and the work needed to operationalize the CWVRs as useful test systems and to make broader progress. We currently lack statistically relevant WVR correction data to understand their expected performance for the ngVLA sites. We also discuss parallel additional approaches alternative and/or complementary to the CWVRs. A path involving tests to establish the functionality, stability, sensitivity, and switched power cal/data synchronism of the CWVR system in the lab and on antenna, followed by on-sky tests, and a parallel effort to assess the utility of autocorrelation outputs of the VLA K-band receivers for delay estimation is recommended. Potential paths going beyond traditional excess path estimation methods which may be necessary to meet ngVLA requirements and goals are touched upon.

</details>


### [115] [Spectral Information Content of Diffraction-Limited Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41092)
*Milo Langker,Hayden Greer,Benjamin Pope,Peter Tuthill*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的框架，用于分析和提取衍射受限光学成像中点扩散函数（PSF）所隐含的光谱信息，揭示了即使在理想无噪声条件下，PSF也包含任意精细的光谱结构，并通过可微正向模型和Fisher信息量化其光谱分辨能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率光学成像受衍射限制，其点扩散函数（PSF）具有固有色度（chromaticity），即PSF随波长变化，从而在空间图像中隐含光谱信息；但传统成像通常忽略或未充分利用这一特性。

Method: 建立PSF色度的理论模型，将多色PSF形成表述为对数坐标下的卷积，定义对应的光谱传递函数（spectral transfer function）；推导Airy盘的该函数并分析其对数频率域衰减特性；利用可微分前向模型数值构建光谱信息本征基，并结合Fisher信息量化不同噪声与探测器系统误差下的光谱分辨能力。

Result: 发现理想无噪声Airy PSF的光谱传递函数呈代数衰减，意味着其蕴含任意精细的光谱结构；数值构造出光谱信息本征基，并在真实感模拟中成功注入并恢复受约束的光谱模式，性能优于已有方法。

Conclusion: 该信息论框架为各类成像场景下PSF的光谱表征提供了通用、可推广的方法，拓展了传统成像的信息维度，为计算光学与光谱超分辨提供新思路。

Abstract: High-resolution optical imaging is fundamentally limited by diffraction, which imprints wavelength-dependent structure onto the point spread function (PSF) of the optical system. This intrinsic chromaticity implies that in addition to spatial structure, images encode spectral information. We develop the theory of this spectral information content and provide strategies for its optimal extraction with calculations of Fisher information. We show that polychromatic PSF formation can be expressed as a convolution in logarithmic coordinates and define the corresponding spectral transfer function. We derive this function for the Airy disk and find algebraic decay in spectral log-frequency, implying the existence of arbitrarily fine spectral structure in the ideal, noiseless PSF. We use a differentiable forward model to numerically construct the spectral information eigenbasis and quantify the spectroscopic capability of diffraction-limited imaging across a range of noise and detector systematics. We demonstrate injection and recovery of the constrained spectral modes in a realistic simulated example and compare these results with previously reported methods. Our information-theoretic framework provides a generalisable method for spectral characterisation of PSFs spanning a variety of imaging contexts.

</details>


### [116] [Cryogenic Optical Characteristics of S-FPL51 and S-LAH71: Refractive index, Transmission and Thermal expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41481)
*J. A. van den Born,E. Elswijk,E. Dijkstra,P. van Nijnatten,A. W. Janssen,R. Navarro*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了OHARA S-FPL51和S-LAH71玻璃在低温（液氮温区）下的光学与机械性能，包括折射率、透射率和热膨胀系数的测量，为未来大型望远镜近红外仪器的设计提供关键材料参数支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因下一代极大望远镜的近红外仪器需在液氮温度下运行，且采用S-FPL51与S-LAH71玻璃棱镜校正大气差分折射，故需准确掌握其低温下的光学与机械性能以支撑设计。

Method: 在293 K、100 K、80 K测量折射率；在293 K、77 K、43 K测量透射率；在293 K至45 K范围内测定热膨胀系数；并描述所用低温测试装置及拟合得到的物性参数方程。

Result: 获得了S-FPL51和S-LAH71玻璃在低温下的系统性折射率、透射率和热膨胀系数数据，并给出了相应的经验拟合方程。

Conclusion: 该低温物性表征结果可直接用于未来大型望远镜近红外仪器的概念设计权衡、机械接口设计及精细光学设计。

Abstract: Several near-infrared instruments on upcoming extremely large telescopes use a combination of OHARA S-FPL51 and S-LAH71 glass prisms to correct for the atmospheric differential refraction. Because large parts of these instruments are contained within a vessel cooled to liquid nitrogen temperatures, the changes in optical and mechanical properties should be considered during the design phase.
  In this report, we provide an update to our continuing efforts to characterize the S-FPL51 and S-LAH71 glass under cryogenic conditions. These efforts include a measurement of the refractive index (293 K, 100 K and 80 K), transmission measurements (293 K, 77 K and 43 K) and a determination of the coefficient of thermal expansion (between 293 K and 45 K). We will describe the cryogenic test setups that we have used to obtain our results, and provide summarizing equations of these material properties.
  This characterization will prove useful for conceptual design trade-offs, mechanical interface solutions and detailed optical design for future near-infrared instrumentation on large telescopes.

</details>


### [117] [Demographics and growth of the astronomy and astrophysics community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41535)
*Jürgen Knödlseder*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文基于ADS数据，分析了过去200年天文学与天体物理学界的人口统计与增长趋势，发现该领域正以每年4.3%（近十年加速至5.1%）指数增长，2025年全球天文学家约10万人，其中中国和印度增长最快，预计将在2029和2043年成为全球第一、第二大天文群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解天文学与天体物理学社区的长期发展规模、地域分布与结构特征，为科研政策制定与资源分配提供依据。

Method: 基于美国天体物理数据系统（ADS）的历史文献作者数据，进行人口统计学分析，包括作者数量估算、地域分布、增长率建模（指数拟合）、人均与GDP归一化分析，并与地球科学、物理学等学科对比。

Result: 2025年全球天文学家约10万人（其中活跃作者59940人），欧洲占38%、亚洲29%、北美26%；社区以青年学者为主（50%发表年限≤7年）；年均增长4.3%（近十年达5.1%），中国和印度分别达13%和10%；人均论文量呈下降趋势；中国和印度将分别于2029年和2043年成为最大和第二大天文群体。

Conclusion: 天文学界正经历快速且持续的全球化扩张，尤其由亚洲新兴科研力量驱动；其增长速度超过论文产出增速，提示合作深化与分工细化趋势；未来格局将显著向亚太倾斜。

Abstract: This paper presents the demographics and growth of the astronomy and astrophysics community over the past ~200 years based on data provided by the Astrophysics Data System. The analysis suggests that 59940 authors signed at least one paper in 2025, while about 40% of the community did not publish any papers, resulting in an estimated community size of roughly 100000 astronomers in 2025. About 38% of the community is hosted in Europe, followed by Asia (29%), North America (26%), South America (4%), Oceania (2%) and Africa (1%). Large variations exist among the countries in the number of astronomers, astronomers per capita of country population and astronomers per country gross domestic product (GDP). Overall, the community is dominated by early career scientists, with 50% of the authors publishing papers for seven years or less. This dominance is readily explained by a steady and exponential growth of the community of 4.3% per year over the last 50 years, implying that the community-size doubled every 16 years. The growth rate of the community exceeds that of the number of papers or paper pages, implying that the annual number of papers or pages published per astronomer is on decline. Over the last decade, the exponential growth of the community has accelerated to 5.1% per year, owing in particular to countries like China (13% per year) and India (10% per year). For almost all countries in the world, growth rates in authors, authors per capita and authors per GDP are positive. The growth in astronomy is not an exception, as the growth rates of the earth sciences and physics communities are even larger. If the past tendencies continue, Chinese and Indian astronomers will be first and second largest communities in the world by 2029 and 2043, respectively.

</details>


### [118] [HWP-synchronous signal induced by misalignment of the sapphire crystal axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41586)
*Yurika Hoshino,Ryota Takaku,Tomotake Matsumura,Yuki Sakurai,Nobuhiko Katayama,Makoto Tashiro,Yukikatsu Terada,Kosuke Sato,Satoru Katsuda,Tommaso Ghigna,Kunimoto Komatsu,Shinya Sugiyama*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转蓝宝石半波片（HWP）在光轴偏斜（即偏离理想a-cut取向）情况下的光学响应，扩展了考虑光轴偏斜与斜入射的多层波片传输矩阵模型，并在90 GHz下实验验证；发现偏斜与斜入射分别引入偶次谐波，二者共存时还产生奇次谐波；3度小偏斜在单层HWP中引起约1%奇次谐波，在五层消色差HWP中可达8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确极化测量中，蓝宝石波片的光轴偏斜可能引入系统误差，需定量建模其对调制响应（尤其是谐波成分）的影响。

Method: 扩展传输矩阵形式主义以纳入光轴偏斜和斜入射效应，理论计算旋转HWP对线偏振光的调制透射率，并在90 GHz开展实验验证。

Result: 理论与实验一致：光轴偏斜和斜入射各自激发偶次谐波，二者叠加时出现奇次谐波；3度偏斜在单层HWP中导致~1%奇次谐波幅度，在五层消色差HWP中个别频率达8%。

Conclusion: 光轴偏斜是高精度极化测量中不可忽略的误差源，尤其在多层消色差波片中影响显著；所提模型可有效预测并校正此类系统误差。

Abstract: We investigate the optical response of a rotating sapphire half-wave plate (HWP) with optic axis misalignment, defined as a deviation of the optic axis from the ideal a-cut orientation. We extend the transfer-matrix formalism for calculating the modulated transmittance of multi-layer wave plates with optic axis misalignment and oblique incidence. Using this formalism, we compute the modulated response of a rotating HWP with an optic axis misalignment to linearly polarized incident light. We experimentally validate the model at 90 GHz using sapphire wave plates with intentionally introduced optic axis misalignments of up to 30 degrees. Additional even harmonics are observed for oblique incidence and optic axis misalignment, with odd harmonics appearing when both effects are present. The measured modulation signals are in good agreement with the model predictions. We also evaluate the potential impact of a illustrative small optic axis misalignment of 3 degrees on precision polarimetry. For the configuration considered, the model predicts odd-harmonic amplitudes at the percent level for a single-layer HWP and up to 8% at individual frequencies for a five-layer achromatic HWP.

</details>


### [119] [First Light with the Dark100 Array of PANOSETI Telescopes at Palomar Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41775)
*Nikolas Korzoun,Aaron Brown,Stephen Fegan,Luca Fiedler,Benjamin Godfrey,Jamie Holder,Paul Horowitz,Andrew W. Howard,Alyssa Johnson,Wei Liu,Jérôme Maire,Yuriy Popovych,Elisa Pueschel,Nicolas Rault-Wang,Sruthiranjani Ravikularaman,Dan Werthimer,Shelley A. Wright*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: Dark100是PANOSETI项目的第一阶段，利用7台低成本、广域分布的0.5米Fresnel透镜望远镜（配备1024像素SiPM相机）作为成像大气切伦科夫望远镜（IACT），开展超高能伽马射线源巡天与超重暗物质（>100 TeV）探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展IACT技术应用，以低成本实现高分辨率UHE伽马射线巡天，并探索传统IACT难以覆盖的超重暗物质质量区间。

Method: 部署并运行7台基于SiPM的0.5米Fresnel透镜望远镜阵列，采用多站符合触发和大气切伦科夫光成像技术进行UHE伽马射线和暗物质信号探测。

Result: 已完成4台望远镜部署，3台联合观测已积累超100小时数据（含Crab Nebula、Mrk 421等源），后续3台预计2026年底完成调试。

Conclusion: Dark100验证了小型化、低成本IACT阵列在UHE伽马天文与前沿暗物质探测中的可行性，为后续大规模PANOSETI部署奠定基础。

Abstract: Dark100 is the first phase of an extensive array of Panoramic SETI (PANOSETI) telescopes at Palomar Observatory, California. PANOSETI telescopes are small, 0.5-m Fresnel lens telescopes instrumented with 1024-pixel silicon photomultiplier (SiPM) cameras. The telescopes were originally designed to search for ultra-fast optical transients, but they also function as cost-effective imaging atmospheric Cherenkov telescopes (IACTs). Dark100 will use seven of these widely separated IACTs to map the ultra-high-energy (UHE) gamma-ray sources on the Galactic Plane at high resolution. Dark100 will also leverage the IACT technique to search for evidence of ultra-heavy dark matter in a mass range that extends beyond 100 TeV. Deployment of the first four telescopes and enclosures was completed in December 2025, and commissioning of the next three is scheduled to finish by the end of 2026. The array has so far collected more than 100 hours of 3-telescope data on sources including the Crab Nebula and Markarian 421. Here we present the current status of Dark100.

</details>


### [120] [An exploration of in-band spectral index techniques for extended radio sources with EMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41934)
*B. D. Ball,R. Kothes,E. Rosolowsky,J. L. West,W. Raja,M. D. Filipovic,A. M. Hopkins,S. Lazarevic,D. Leahy,S. F. Rahman,Z. J. Smeaton,S. Taziaux*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在单台望远镜观测（EMU/ASKAP）中估算射电源“频内谱指数”的三种方法，并评估其对扩展源（如超新星遗迹和HII区）的可靠性，发现温度-温度（T-T）图法最稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因多频段、多仪器观测数据常不可得，亟需从单次宽带观测中可靠提取频内谱指数，尤其针对扩展射电源；而现有方法的可靠性尚未系统评估。

Method: 在EMU（ASKAP）巡天数据上，对比三种频内谱指数估测方法：(1) 子频带流量积分法，(2) 温度-温度（T-T）图法，(3) 成像所得Taylor展开系数比值法；以超新星遗迹（SNR）和HII区为样本，与已发表的宽频谱指数及理论预期值进行交叉验证。

Result: T-T图法在各类尺寸与亮度的扩展源中表现最可靠；子频带法和Taylor项比值法仅在特定条件下可用，整体可靠性不足。T-T法能较准确区分热辐射（HII区）与非热辐射（SNR）源。

Conclusion: 对于ASKAP等宽带干涉阵的扩展射电源分析，T-T图是当前最推荐的频内谱指数估计方法；其他方法需谨慎使用，不宜泛化。

Abstract: Spectral indices are an effective way of differentiating between thermal and nonthermal sources of radio emission. Most often, spectral indices are determined using flux measurements taken over multiple observations with different instruments. Since these data are not always available, determining spectral indices from a single telescope observation is desirable. The reliability of these in-band spectral indices has not been well studied, particularly for extended sources. Here, we explore three different techniques for finding in-band spectral indices with the Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU), a radio southern sky survey being conducted with the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP). These methods include: (1) integrating fluxes in sub-bands, (2) temperature-temperature (T-T) plots, and (3) using the ratio of the Taylor terms produced in the imaging process. We apply these techniques to a selection of extended sources of radio emission, primarily supernova remnants (SNRs) and HII regions, and assess if the indices produced through these methods agree with the expected values. For the SNRs, we also compare the in-band results to broadband spectral indices determined using flux values obtained from published works. We examine whether and when the in-band techniques can differentiate between thermal and nonthermal sources. In general, we find the T-T plot method to be the most reliable across the range of source sizes and brightnesses studied for both SNRs and HII regions. The sub-band and Taylor term methods can be useful under certain conditions but cannot be considered as broadly reliable for extended sources.

</details>
